## CI Workflow Structure Analysis

Based on my thorough review of all workflow files, here's the complete CI workflow architecture:

### **Workflow Files Overview**

**Location:** `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box-ci-debug/.github/workflows/`

**Relevant workflow files:**
1. **web-e2e.yml** - Web E2E test runner
2. **desktop-e2e.yml** - Desktop E2E test runner (cross-platform)
3. **ci-e2e.yml** - CI orchestrator that triggers E2E workflows
4. **release-gate.yml** - Release verification gate (checks E2E status for releases)
5. **ci.yml** - General CI for PRs (linting, tests, builds)

---

### **1. E2E Test Workflows**

#### **Web E2E (`web-e2e.yml`)**
- **Trigger:** `workflow_dispatch` or `workflow_call` (called by other workflows)
- **Input:** Optional `ref` parameter for git checkout
- **Job name:** `web-e2e`
- **Runs on:** `ubuntu-latest`
- **Timeout:** 20 minutes
- **Services:** PostgreSQL (postgres:16-alpine), IPFS (ipfs/kubo:v0.40.0), Redis (redis:7-alpine)
- **Main test command:** `pnpm --filter @cipherbox/web-e2e test`

#### **Desktop E2E (`desktop-e2e.yml`)**
- **Trigger:** `workflow_dispatch` or `workflow_call` (called by other workflows)
- **Input:** Optional `ref` parameter for git checkout
- **Job name:** `desktop-e2e`
- **Runs on:** Matrix of ubuntu-22.04, macos-latest, windows-latest
- **Timeout:** 45 minutes
- **Key steps:** 
  - Install platform-specific FUSE drivers (FUSE-T on macOS, WinFsp on Windows, libfuse3 on Linux)
  - Build desktop frontend (Vite)
  - Build debug binary (Cargo)
  - Start services (PostgreSQL, IPFS, Redis)
  - Run E2E tests via platform-specific scripts

---

### **2. CI Orchestrator (`ci-e2e.yml`)**

**Purpose:** Orchestrates E2E testing on `main` branch pushes and manual dispatch

**Trigger:** 
- `push` to `main` branch
- `workflow_dispatch` (manual trigger)

**Jobs:**

1. **`detect-changes` job (lines 9-69)**
   - Determines which E2E workflows should run
   - **Output variables:**
     - `web`: true/false (detects changes in web-related paths)
     - `desktop`: true/false (detects changes in desktop-related paths)
   - **Change detection:**
     - **Web paths:** `apps/web/`, `apps/api/`, `packages/`, `tests/web-e2e/`, `tools/mock-ipns-routing/`, `.github/workflows/web-e2e.yml`
     - **Desktop paths:** `apps/desktop/src/`, `apps/desktop/src-tauri/`, `crates/`, `Cargo.toml`, `Cargo.lock`, `tests/vectors/`, `packages/crypto/`, `.github/workflows/desktop-e2e.yml`
   - On manual dispatch: forces **both** `web=true` and `desktop=true`

2. **`web-e2e` job (lines 71-76)**
   - **Condition:** `if: needs.detect-changes.outputs.web == 'true' || github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch'`
   - **Uses:** `./.github/workflows/web-e2e.yml` (calls the web E2E workflow)
   - **Secrets:** Inherited from parent workflow

3. **`desktop-e2e` job (lines 78-83)**
   - **Condition:** `if: needs.detect-changes.outputs.desktop == 'true' || github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch'`
   - **Uses:** `./.github/workflows/desktop-e2e.yml` (calls the desktop E2E workflow)
   - **Secrets:** Inherited from parent workflow

4. **`retrigger-release-gate` job (lines 85-122)**
   - **Purpose:** Re-trigger the release gate workflow after E2E tests complete
   - **Condition:** `if: always() && (github.event_name == 'push' || github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch')`
   - **Logic:** 
     - Finds any open `release-please-*` PR
     - Gets the most recent completed `release-gate.yml` run for that branch
     - Re-runs it so it verifies E2E against the current main

---

### **3. Release Gate (`release-gate.yml`)**

**Purpose:** Verification gate for release PRs - ensures E2E tests pass before release

**Trigger:** `pull_request` to `main` (on opened, synchronize, reopened)

**Key Logic:**

#### **Job 1: `detect-changes` (lines 9-130)**
- **Purpose:** Determines if web/desktop actually changed since last release
- **Output variables:**
  - `web`: true/false
  - `desktop`: true/false
  - `prev_tag`: Git tag of previous release
  - `is_release`: true if this is a release-please PR (branch starts with `release-please--`)
- **For non-release PRs:** Sets all flags to false and skips detection
- **For release PRs:** 
  - Finds the latest non-staging release tag (pattern `cipher-box-v*` or fallback to `v*`)
  - Compares current main against that tag using the exact **same path patterns as ci-e2e.yml**

#### **Job 2: `verify-e2e` (lines 132-371)**
- **Purpose:** Verifies E2E test status for release candidate
- **Key Steps:**

**Web E2E Verification (lines 149-235):**
- **Skip if:** No web changes detected OR not a release PR
- Polls `ci-e2e.yml` runs on main looking for the latest commit
- Waits up to 20 minutes for `ci-e2e.yml` to complete
- Verifies that Web E2E job actually ran (not skipped) and passed
- If Web E2E was skipped in the latest run, searches back through recent CI E2E runs to find one where Web E2E actually executed
- **Job name matching:** Uses `contains("Web E2E")` to match the job name from the workflow

**Desktop E2E Verification (lines 237-358):**
- **Skip if:** No desktop changes detected OR not a release PR
- Checks if there's a Desktop E2E run for the current HEAD commit
- If HEAD run exists and still running: Polls up to 20 minutes for completion
- If no HEAD run exists: Walks commits backwards (newest-first) from the previous tag to current main
- Finds the first Desktop E2E run for any commit in that range
- Waits for that run to complete if needed
- Verifies it passed

**Summary (lines 364-371):**
- Reports which changes were detected and confirms all applicable E2E gates passed

---

### **Critical Distinctions**

**"Web E2E" vs "Desktop E2E" vs "CI E2E":**
- **"Web E2E"** = The actual Playwright-based web app tests (runs in `web-e2e.yml`)
- **"Desktop E2E"** = The cross-platform desktop app tests (runs in `desktop-e2e.yml`)
- **"CI E2E"** = The orchestrator workflow that decides which of the above to run and re-triggers release gate (in `ci-e2e.yml`)

**Gate Workflow Determination:**
The release-gate.yml gate determines which E2E workflows to check by:
1. Detecting changes **using the same path patterns as ci-e2e.yml**
2. Only requiring E2E verification for components that actually changed
3. For Web E2E: Polling `ci-e2e.yml` runs and checking job-level results via GitHub API
4. For Desktop E2E: Polling `desktop-e2e.yml` runs directly (whether standalone or called by ci-e2e.yml)

---

### **File Paths**
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box-ci-debug/.github/workflows/web-e2e.yml`
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box-ci-debug/.github/workflows/desktop-e2e.yml`
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box-ci-debug/.github/workflows/ci-e2e.yml`
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box-ci-debug/.github/workflows/release-gate.yml`
- `/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box-ci-debug/.github/workflows/ci.yml`

Now I have the complete picture. Here is the analysis:

---

## Root Cause

The **"Release Gate"** workflow (run `23725025314`) failed at the **"Verify Desktop E2E (if desktop changed)"** step because **no Desktop E2E run exists for any commit in the range `cipher-box-v0.32.0..HEAD`**.

### What happened step by step:

1. **The Release Gate** (triggered by the Release Please PR on branch `release-please--branches--main`) runs a "Verify E2E Passed" job that checks both Web and Desktop E2E results.

2. **Change detection** correctly found that both web and desktop files changed since the last release tag `cipher-box-v0.32.0` (specifically, changes to `.github/workflows/web-e2e.yml` and `.github/workflows/desktop-e2e.yml`).

3. **Web E2E passed** -- the CI E2E orchestrator (run `23725015687`) completed successfully and the Web E2E job within it passed.

4. **Desktop E2E failed verification** -- the gate script:
   - First looked for a Desktop E2E run at HEAD (`bb87d17...`) -- none found.
   - Then walked all 6 commits in the range `cipher-box-v0.32.0..HEAD` looking for any `desktop-e2e.yml` run matching those SHAs -- none matched.
   - Result: `"No Desktop E2E run found for any commit in cipher-box-v0.32.0..HEAD."`

5. **The successful Desktop E2E run you're aware of** (run `23720651610`) ran at SHA `a595e4b5...` via `workflow_dispatch`. That SHA is **not in the commit range** `cipher-box-v0.32.0..HEAD`. The 6 commits in range are:
   - `b03f8a3...`
   - `10bf6fa...` (use --cvm-id flag)
   - `4e7fb92...` (bump ssh-action)
   - `28d1390...` (skip E2E gates)
   - `0a8d740...` (bump scp-action)
   - `bb87d17...` (force all E2E suites) -- HEAD

6. The Desktop E2E runs that **do** exist on main all have SHAs from earlier commits (`78bef4b...`, `a9604a1...`, etc.) which are all **before** `cipher-box-v0.32.0`, so they fall outside the range.

### In summary

The Desktop E2E workflow is being triggered by `push` events for older commits but **not** for the 6 recent CI-only commits (which only touch `.github/workflows/` files). The `ci-e2e.yml` orchestrator appears to conditionally call `desktop-e2e.yml` only when desktop source files change, and these 6 commits only changed workflow YAML files. However, the Release Gate's change detection uses broad path patterns that include `.github/workflows/desktop-e2e.yml` itself as a "desktop change," creating a mismatch: the gate **requires** a Desktop E2E run, but the orchestrator **didn't trigger** one because the actual desktop source code didn't change.

### Fix options

The simplest fix is one of:
1. **Exclude workflow YAML files** from the Release Gate's desktop/web change detection patterns (since workflow-only changes don't need E2E re-verification).
2. **Have the gate accept a recent successful Desktop E2E run from before the range** when the only "desktop changes" are to CI workflow files, not actual source.
3. **Manually trigger a Desktop E2E run at HEAD** (`bb87d17...`) to unblock this specific release, then fix the pattern mismatch to prevent recurrence.

230	              exit 1
231	            fi
232	            echo "Web E2E: passed (in CI E2E run ${RECENT_RUN_ID})"
233	          else
234	            echo "Web E2E: passed (at HEAD)"
235	          fi
236	
237	      - name: Verify Desktop E2E (if desktop changed)
238	        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--') && needs.detect-changes.outputs.desktop == 'true'
239	        env:
240	          GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
241	          PREV_TAG: ${{ needs.detect-changes.outputs.prev_tag }}
242	        run: |
243	          MAIN_SHA=$(gh api repos/${{ github.repository }}/git/ref/heads/main --jq '.object.sha')
244	          echo "Desktop changes detected since ${PREV_TAG} — verifying Desktop E2E"
245	
246	          # Search desktop-e2e.yml runs directly — covers both standalone triggers
247	          # and orchestrator-called runs (workflow name is the same either way).
248	
249	          # First: check if there's a run for HEAD (may still be running)
250	          HEAD_RUN=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
251	            --workflow=desktop-e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=10 \
252	            --json headSha,conclusion,status,databaseId \
253	            --jq "[.[] | select(.headSha == \"${MAIN_SHA}\")][0]")
254	
255	          HEAD_STATUS=$(echo "$HEAD_RUN" | jq -r '.status // empty')
256	
257	          if [ -n "$HEAD_STATUS" ] && [ "$HEAD_STATUS" != "null" ]; then
258	            HEAD_RUN_ID=$(echo "$HEAD_RUN" | jq -r '.databaseId')
259	            echo "Desktop E2E run exists for HEAD (run ${HEAD_RUN_ID}) — polling until complete..."
260	            MAX_ATTEMPTS=40
261	            POLL_INTERVAL=30
262	
263	            for attempt in $(seq 1 $MAX_ATTEMPTS); do
264	              CONCLUSION=$(gh run view "$HEAD_RUN_ID" --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
265	                --json conclusion --jq '.conclusion // empty')
266	
267	              if [ -n "$CONCLUSION" ]; then
268	                if [ "$CONCLUSION" == "success" ]; then
269	                  echo "Desktop E2E: passed (at HEAD, run ${HEAD_RUN_ID})"
270	                  exit 0
271	                else
272	                  echo "::error::Desktop E2E at HEAD did not pass (conclusion: ${CONCLUSION}, run ${HEAD_RUN_ID})"
273	                  exit 1
274	                fi
275	              fi
276	
277	              if [ "$attempt" -eq "$MAX_ATTEMPTS" ]; then
278	                echo "::error::Desktop E2E run did not complete within $(( MAX_ATTEMPTS * POLL_INTERVAL / 60 )) minutes."
279	                exit 1
280	              fi
281	
282	              echo "Attempt ${attempt}/${MAX_ATTEMPTS}: Desktop E2E still running, waiting ${POLL_INTERVAL}s..."
283	              sleep $POLL_INTERVAL
284	            done
285	          fi
286	
287	          # No run for HEAD — walk commits newest-first and find a Desktop E2E run
288	          echo "No Desktop E2E run for HEAD. Searching since ${PREV_TAG}..."
289	
290	          if [ -z "$PREV_TAG" ]; then
291	            echo "::error::No previous release tag and no Desktop E2E run for HEAD."
292	            exit 1
293	          fi
294	
295	          # Commits newest-first (reverse the API's oldest-first order)
296	          RANGE_COMMITS=$(gh api "repos/${{ github.repository }}/compare/${PREV_TAG}...${MAIN_SHA}" \
297	            --jq '[.commits[].sha] | reverse | .[]' 2>/dev/null || echo "")
298	
299	          if [ -z "$RANGE_COMMITS" ]; then
300	            echo "::error::Could not determine commits in range ${PREV_TAG}..${MAIN_SHA}"
301	            exit 1
302	          fi
303	
304	          # Build a lookup of all desktop-e2e runs (includes in-progress)
305	          RUNS=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
306	            --workflow=desktop-e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=50 \
307	            --json headSha,conclusion,status,databaseId \
308	            --jq '.[] | "\(.headSha) \(.status) \(.conclusion) \(.databaseId)"')
309	
310	          FOUND_SHA=""
311	          FOUND_CONCLUSION=""
312	          FOUND_RUN_ID=""
313	
314	          # Walk commits newest-first, find the first one with a desktop-e2e run
315	          while IFS= read -r commit_sha; do
316	            [ -z "$commit_sha" ] && continue
317	            RUN_LINE=$(echo "$RUNS" | grep "^${commit_sha} " | head -1)
318	            [ -z "$RUN_LINE" ] && continue
319	
320	            read -r _sha run_status run_conclusion run_id <<< "$RUN_LINE"
321	            echo "Found Desktop E2E run ${run_id} for ${commit_sha} (status: ${run_status}, conclusion: ${run_conclusion})"
322	
323	            # If still running, poll until complete
324	            if [ "$run_status" != "completed" ]; then
325	              echo "Run ${run_id} still in progress — polling..."
326	              for attempt in $(seq 1 40); do
327	                run_conclusion=$(gh run view "$run_id" --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
328	                  --json conclusion --jq '.conclusion // empty')
329	                if [ -n "$run_conclusion" ]; then
330	                  echo "Run ${run_id} completed (conclusion: ${run_conclusion})"
331	                  break
332	                fi
333	                if [ "$attempt" -eq 40 ]; then
334	                  echo "::error::Desktop E2E run ${run_id} did not complete within 20 minutes."
335	                  exit 1
336	                fi
337	                echo "Attempt ${attempt}/40: still running, waiting 30s..."
338	                sleep 30
339	              done
340	            fi
341	
342	            FOUND_SHA="$commit_sha"
343	            FOUND_CONCLUSION="$run_conclusion"
344	            FOUND_RUN_ID="$run_id"
345	            break
346	          done <<< "$RANGE_COMMITS"
347	
348	          if [ -z "$FOUND_SHA" ]; then
349	            echo "::error::No Desktop E2E run found for any commit in ${PREV_TAG}..HEAD."
350	            exit 1
351	          fi
352	
353	          if [ "$FOUND_CONCLUSION" != "success" ]; then
354	            echo "::error::Most recent Desktop E2E run in range (at ${FOUND_SHA}) did not succeed (conclusion: ${FOUND_CONCLUSION}, run ${FOUND_RUN_ID})."
355	            exit 1
356	          fi
357	
358	          echo "Desktop E2E: passed (run ${FOUND_RUN_ID} at ${FOUND_SHA})"
359	
360	      - name: Skip Desktop E2E (no desktop changes)
361	        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--') && needs.detect-changes.outputs.desktop != 'true'
362	        run: echo "No desktop-related changes since last release — skipping Desktop E2E gate"
363	
364	      - name: Summary
365	        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--')
366	        env:
367	          WEB_CHANGED: ${{ needs.detect-changes.outputs.web }}
368	          DESKTOP_CHANGED: ${{ needs.detect-changes.outputs.desktop }}
369	        run: |
370	          echo "Web changes: ${WEB_CHANGED}, Desktop changes: ${DESKTOP_CHANGED}"
371	          echo "All applicable E2E gates passed — release is safe to merge."
372	
```

> AGENT

I now have a clear picture of both issues:

**Issue 1 - Web E2E check**: Lines 194-197 check the CI E2E *overall* conclusion before checking the Web E2E job. If Desktop E2E fails, the orchestrator fails, and the web check fails even though the Web E2E job itself passed.

**Issue 2 - Desktop E2E check**: Lines 250-253 search for standalone `desktop-e2e.yml` workflow runs. But when ci-e2e.yml calls desktop-e2e.yml via `workflow_call`, it doesn't create separate workflow runs — the desktop jobs appear as jobs *within* the ci-e2e.yml run. So the gate never finds them.

Let me fix both.

> TOOL

> AGENT

Here's how each scenario works with the current/planned code:

**ci-e2e.yml dispatched on main**: **Yes** — both the web and desktop checks filter by `--workflow=ci-e2e.yml --branch=main` and match on `headSha`. A manual dispatch on main would be found (its `headSha` = main's HEAD at dispatch time).

**Standalone web-e2e.yml dispatched**: **No** — the web check only looks at ci-e2e.yml runs and checks for the "Web E2E" job within them. A standalone `web-e2e.yml` dispatch is a completely separate workflow run and would never be found.

**Standalone desktop-e2e.yml dispatched**: **No** after the rewrite — I'm changing desktop to check ci-e2e.yml jobs (same pattern as web). The old code searched for `desktop-e2e.yml` runs directly, but that's what broke because `workflow_call` runs don't appear there. A standalone dispatch would, but that's coincidental.

So the safest manual unblock path is to dispatch `ci-e2e.yml` on main — that triggers both suites and the gate will find them. But if you want standalone dispatches to also work as a fallback, I should add that. Want me to include fallback checks for standalone `web-e2e.yml` and `desktop-e2e.yml` runs at HEAD (after checking ci-e2e.yml jobs first)?

> DEVELOPER

243	
244	          # Search desktop-e2e.yml runs directly — covers both standalone triggers
245	          # and orchestrator-called runs (workflow name is the same either way).
246	
247	          # First: check if there's a run for HEAD (may still be running)
248	          HEAD_RUN=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
249	            --workflow=desktop-e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=10 \
250	            --json headSha,conclusion,status,databaseId \
251	            --jq "[.[] | select(.headSha == \"${MAIN_SHA}\")][0]")
252	
253	          HEAD_STATUS=$(echo "$HEAD_RUN" | jq -r '.status // empty')
254	
255	          if [ -n "$HEAD_STATUS" ] && [ "$HEAD_STATUS" != "null" ]; then
256	            HEAD_RUN_ID=$(echo "$HEAD_RUN" | jq -r '.databaseId')
257	            echo "Desktop E2E run exists for HEAD (run ${HEAD_RUN_ID}) — polling until complete..."
258	            MAX_ATTEMPTS=40
259	            POLL_INTERVAL=30
260	
261	            for attempt in $(seq 1 $MAX_ATTEMPTS); do
262	              CONCLUSION=$(gh run view "$HEAD_RUN_ID" --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
263	                --json conclusion --jq '.conclusion // empty')
264	
265	              if [ -n "$CONCLUSION" ]; then
266	                if [ "$CONCLUSION" == "success" ]; then
267	                  echo "Desktop E2E: passed (at HEAD, run ${HEAD_RUN_ID})"
268	                  exit 0
269	                else
270	                  echo "::error::Desktop E2E at HEAD did not pass (conclusion: ${CONCLUSION}, run ${HEAD_RUN_ID})"
271	                  exit 1
272	                fi
273	              fi
274	
275	              if [ "$attempt" -eq "$MAX_ATTEMPTS" ]; then
276	                echo "::error::Desktop E2E run did not complete within $(( MAX_ATTEMPTS * POLL_INTERVAL / 60 )) minutes."
277	                exit 1
278	              fi
279	
280	              echo "Attempt ${attempt}/${MAX_ATTEMPTS}: Desktop E2E still running, waiting ${POLL_INTERVAL}s..."
281	              sleep $POLL_INTERVAL
282	            done
283	          fi
284	
285	          # No run for HEAD — walk commits newest-first and find a Desktop E2E run
286	          echo "No Desktop E2E run for HEAD. Searching since ${PREV_TAG}..."
287	
288	          if [ -z "$PREV_TAG" ]; then
289	            echo "::error::No previous release tag and no Desktop E2E run for HEAD."
290	            exit 1
291	          fi
292	
293	          # Commits newest-first (reverse the API's oldest-first order)
294	          RANGE_COMMITS=$(gh api "repos/${{ github.repository }}/compare/${PREV_TAG}...${MAIN_SHA}" \
295	            --jq '[.commits[].sha] | reverse | .[]' 2>/dev/null || echo "")
296	
297	          if [ -z "$RANGE_COMMITS" ]; then
298	            echo "::error::Could not determine commits in range ${PREV_TAG}..${MAIN_SHA}"
299	            exit 1
300	          fi
301	
302	          # Build a lookup of all desktop-e2e runs (includes in-progress)
303	          RUNS=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
304	            --workflow=desktop-e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=50 \
305	            --json headSha,conclusion,status,databaseId \
306	            --jq '.[] | "\(.headSha) \(.status) \(.conclusion) \(.databaseId)"')
307	
308	          FOUND_SHA=""
309	          FOUND_CONCLUSION=""
310	          FOUND_RUN_ID=""
311	
312	          # Walk commits newest-first, find the first one with a desktop-e2e run
313	          while IFS= read -r commit_sha; do
314	            [ -z "$commit_sha" ] && continue
315	            RUN_LINE=$(echo "$RUNS" | grep "^${commit_sha} " | head -1)
316	            [ -z "$RUN_LINE" ] && continue
317	
318	            read -r _sha run_status run_conclusion run_id <<< "$RUN_LINE"
319	            echo "Found Desktop E2E run ${run_id} for ${commit_sha} (status: ${run_status}, conclusion: ${run_conclusion})"
320	
321	            # If still running, poll until complete
322	            if [ "$run_status" != "completed" ]; then
323	              echo "Run ${run_id} still in progress — polling..."
324	              for attempt in $(seq 1 40); do
325	                run_conclusion=$(gh run view "$run_id" --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
326	                  --json conclusion --jq '.conclusion // empty')
327	                if [ -n "$run_conclusion" ]; then
328	                  echo "Run ${run_id} completed (conclusion: ${run_conclusion})"
329	                  break
330	                fi
331	                if [ "$attempt" -eq 40 ]; then
332	                  echo "::error::Desktop E2E run ${run_id} did not complete within 20 minutes."
333	                  exit 1
334	                fi
335	                echo "Attempt ${attempt}/40: still running, waiting 30s..."
336	                sleep 30
337	              done
338	            fi
339	
340	            FOUND_SHA="$commit_sha"
341	            FOUND_CONCLUSION="$run_conclusion"
342	            FOUND_RUN_ID="$run_id"
343	            break
344	          done <<< "$RANGE_COMMITS"
345	
346	          if [ -z "$FOUND_SHA" ]; then
347	            echo "::error::No Desktop E2E run found for any commit in ${PREV_TAG}..HEAD."
348	            exit 1
349	          fi
350	
351	          if [ "$FOUND_CONCLUSION" != "success" ]; then
352	            echo "::error::Most recent Desktop E2E run in range (at ${FOUND_SHA}) did not succeed (conclusion: ${FOUND_CONCLUSION}, run ${FOUND_RUN_ID})."
353	            exit 1
354	          fi
355	
356	          echo "Desktop E2E: passed (run ${FOUND_RUN_ID} at ${FOUND_SHA})"
357	
358	      - name: Skip Desktop E2E (no desktop changes)
359	        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--') && needs.detect-changes.outputs.desktop != 'true'
360	        run: echo "No desktop-related changes since last release — skipping Desktop E2E gate"
361	
362	      - name: Summary
363	        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--')
364	        env:

```
1	name: Release Gate
2	
3	on:
4	  pull_request:
5	    branches: [main]
6	    types: [opened, synchronize, reopened]
7	
8	jobs:
9	  detect-changes:
10	    name: Detect Changes Since Last Release
11	    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
12	    outputs:
13	      web: ${{ steps.check.outputs.web || steps.release-check.outputs.web }}
14	      desktop: ${{ steps.check.outputs.desktop || steps.release-check.outputs.desktop }}
15	      prev_tag: ${{ steps.check.outputs.prev_tag || steps.release-check.outputs.prev_tag }}
16	      is_release: ${{ steps.release-check.outputs.is_release }}
17	    steps:
18	      - name: Check if release PR
19	        id: release-check
20	        env:
21	          HEAD_REF: ${{ github.head_ref }}
22	        run: |
23	          if [[ "$HEAD_REF" == release-please--* ]]; then
24	            echo "is_release=true" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
25	          else
26	            echo "is_release=false" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
27	            echo "web=false" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
28	            echo "desktop=false" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
29	            echo "prev_tag=" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
30	            echo "Not a release PR — skipping change detection"
31	          fi
32	
33	      - uses: actions/checkout@v6
34	        if: steps.release-check.outputs.is_release == 'true'
35	        with:
36	          fetch-depth: 0
37	
38	      - name: Check for web/desktop changes since last release
39	        if: steps.release-check.outputs.is_release == 'true'
40	        id: check
41	        run: |
42	          # Find the latest non-staging release tag (cipher-box-v* format)
43	          PREV_TAG=$(git tag --list 'cipher-box-v*' --sort=-v:refname \
44	            | grep -v 'staging' \
45	            | head -1)
46	          # Fallback to legacy v* tags if no cipher-box-v* tags exist
47	          if [ -z "$PREV_TAG" ]; then
48	            PREV_TAG=$(git tag --list 'v*' --sort=-v:refname \
49	              | grep -v 'staging' \
50	              | grep -v '@' \
51	              | head -1)
52	          fi
53	
54	          if [ -z "$PREV_TAG" ]; then
55	            echo "No previous release tag found — assuming all changed"
56	            echo "web=true" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
57	            echo "desktop=true" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
58	            echo "prev_tag=" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
59	            exit 0
60	          fi
61	
62	          echo "Previous release tag: ${PREV_TAG}"
63	          echo "prev_tag=${PREV_TAG}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
64	          # Use origin/main (not HEAD) so that gh-run-rerun'd workflows
65	          # compare against the current main, not a stale GITHUB_SHA.
66	          git fetch origin main
67	          MAIN_REF="origin/main"
68	          echo "Diffing ${PREV_TAG}..${MAIN_REF}"
69	
70	          # Desktop-related path patterns (based on ci-e2e.yml, excluding
71	          # the ci-e2e.yml orchestrator itself)
72	          DESKTOP_PATTERNS=(
73	            'apps/desktop/src/'
74	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/'
75	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/vendor/'
76	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/capabilities/'
77	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/resources/'
78	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/Cargo.toml'
79	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/build.rs'
80	            'apps/desktop/src-tauri/rust-toolchain.toml'
81	            'apps/desktop/index.html'
82	            'apps/desktop/vite.config.'
83	            'apps/desktop/tsconfig'
84	            'crates/'
85	            'Cargo.toml'
86	            'Cargo.lock'
87	            'tests/vectors/'
88	            'packages/crypto/src/'
89	            'packages/crypto/tsconfig'
90	            '.github/workflows/desktop-e2e.yml'
91	          )
92	
93	          CHANGED_FILES=$(git diff --name-only "${PREV_TAG}..${MAIN_REF}")
94	
95	          # Web-related path patterns (based on ci-e2e.yml, excluding the
96	          # ci-e2e.yml orchestrator itself — changes to the orchestrator
97	          # workflow don't mean the web app changed)
98	          WEB_PATTERNS=(
99	            'apps/web/'
100	            'apps/api/'
101	            'packages/'
102	            'tests/web-e2e/'
103	            'tools/mock-ipns-routing/'
104	            '.github/workflows/web-e2e.yml'
105	          )
106	
107	          WEB_CHANGED=false
108	          for pattern in "${WEB_PATTERNS[@]}"; do
109	            if echo "$CHANGED_FILES" | grep -Fq "$pattern"; then
110	              echo "Web change detected matching pattern: ${pattern}"
111	              WEB_CHANGED=true
112	              break
113	            fi
114	          done
115	
116	          echo "web=${WEB_CHANGED}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
117	          echo "Web changes detected: ${WEB_CHANGED}"
118	
119	          DESKTOP_CHANGED=false
120	
121	          for pattern in "${DESKTOP_PATTERNS[@]}"; do
122	            if echo "$CHANGED_FILES" | grep -Fq "$pattern"; then
123	              echo "Desktop change detected matching pattern: ${pattern}"
124	              DESKTOP_CHANGED=true
125	              break
126	            fi
127	          done
128	
129	          echo "desktop=${DESKTOP_CHANGED}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
130	          echo "Desktop changes detected: ${DESKTOP_CHANGED}"
131	
132	  verify-e2e:
133	    name: Verify E2E Passed
134	    needs: detect-changes
135	    if: ${{ always() && !cancelled() }}
136	    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
137	    timeout-minutes: 40
138	    steps:
139	      - name: Fail if detect-changes did not complete (release PR)
140	        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--') && needs.detect-changes.result != 'success'
141	        run: |
142	          echo "::error::detect-changes must succeed before E2E verification."
143	          exit 1
144	
145	      - name: Skip (not a release PR)
146	        if: "!startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--')"
147	        run: echo "Not a release PR — E2E gate not applicable"
148	
149	      - name: Skip Web E2E (no web changes)
150	        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--') && needs.detect-changes.outputs.web != 'true'
151	        run: echo "No web-related changes since last release — skipping Web E2E gate"
152	
153	      - name: Wait for Web E2E and verify it passed
154	        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--') && needs.detect-changes.outputs.web == 'true'
155	        env:
156	          GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
157	        run: |
158	          # Get the latest commit on main (the base the release PR targets)
159	          MAIN_SHA=$(gh api repos/${{ github.repository }}/git/ref/heads/main --jq '.object.sha')
160	          echo "Latest main commit: ${MAIN_SHA}"
161	
162	          # Poll for the CI E2E orchestrator run to complete (it triggers on the same push)
163	          # 40 * 30s = 20 min — matches the web-e2e.yml job timeout
164	          MAX_ATTEMPTS=40
165	          POLL_INTERVAL=30
166	          echo "Waiting for CI E2E run to complete (polling every ${POLL_INTERVAL}s, max ${MAX_ATTEMPTS} attempts)..."
167	
168	          for attempt in $(seq 1 $MAX_ATTEMPTS); do
169	            E2E_CONCLUSION=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
170	              --workflow=ci-e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=10 \
171	              --json headSha,conclusion,status,databaseId \
172	              --jq "[.[] | select(.headSha == \"${MAIN_SHA}\")][0].conclusion // empty")
173	
174	            if [ -n "$E2E_CONCLUSION" ]; then
175	              echo "CI E2E completed with conclusion: ${E2E_CONCLUSION}"
176	
177	              # Grab the run ID for job-level checks below
178	              E2E_RUN_ID=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
179	                --workflow=ci-e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=10 \
180	                --json headSha,databaseId \
181	                --jq "[.[] | select(.headSha == \"${MAIN_SHA}\")][0].databaseId")
182	              break
183	            fi
184	
185	            if [ "$attempt" -eq "$MAX_ATTEMPTS" ]; then
186	              echo "::error::CI E2E run did not complete within $(( MAX_ATTEMPTS * POLL_INTERVAL / 60 )) minutes for main (${MAIN_SHA})."
187	              exit 1
188	            fi
189	
190	            echo "Attempt ${attempt}/${MAX_ATTEMPTS}: CI E2E still running, waiting ${POLL_INTERVAL}s..."
191	            sleep $POLL_INTERVAL
192	          done
193	
194	          # Don't check overall CI E2E conclusion — it may fail due to
195	          # Desktop E2E issues while Web E2E passed. Check the job directly.
196	
197	          # Verify the Web E2E job actually ran (not skipped due to no web changes).
198	          # When ci-e2e.yml calls web-e2e.yml via workflow_call, the job name is
199	          # composed as "Web E2E / Web E2E Tests" — contains("Web E2E") matches both.
200	          if ! WEB_E2E_RAN=$(gh api "repos/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${E2E_RUN_ID}/jobs" \
201	            --jq '[.jobs[] | select(.name | contains("Web E2E")) | select(.conclusion == "success")] | length'); then
202	            echo "::error::Failed to query jobs for CI E2E run ${E2E_RUN_ID} (API error)"
203	            exit 1
204	          fi
205	
206	          if [ "${WEB_E2E_RAN:-0}" -eq 0 ]; then
207	            echo "CI E2E passed but Web E2E was skipped (no web changes in latest push)."
208	            echo "Searching for most recent CI E2E run where Web E2E actually executed..."
209	
210	            RECENT_RUN_ID=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
211	              --workflow=ci-e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=20 \
212	              --json databaseId,status \
213	              --jq '[.[] | select(.status == "completed")] | .[].databaseId' \
214	              | while read -r rid; do
215	                  if ! WEB_COUNT=$(gh api "repos/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${rid}/jobs" \
216	                    --jq '[.jobs[] | select(.name | contains("Web E2E")) | select(.conclusion == "success")] | length'); then
217	                    echo "::warning::Failed to query jobs for run ${rid}, skipping" >&2
218	                    continue
219	                  fi
220	                  if [ "${WEB_COUNT:-0}" -gt 0 ]; then
221	                    echo "$rid"
222	                    break
223	                  fi
224	                done)
225	
226	            if [ -z "$RECENT_RUN_ID" ]; then
227	              echo "::error::No recent CI E2E run found where Web E2E tests actually executed."
228	              exit 1
229	            fi
230	            echo "Web E2E: passed (in CI E2E run ${RECENT_RUN_ID})"
231	          else
232	            echo "Web E2E: passed (at HEAD)"
233	          fi
234	
235	      - name: Verify Desktop E2E (if desktop changed)
236	        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--') && needs.detect-changes.outputs.desktop == 'true'
237	        env:
238	          GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
239	          PREV_TAG: ${{ needs.detect-changes.outputs.prev_tag }}
240	        run: |
241	          MAIN_SHA=$(gh api repos/${{ github.repository }}/git/ref/heads/main --jq '.object.sha')
242	          echo "Desktop changes detected since ${PREV_TAG} — verifying Desktop E2E"
243	
244	          # Desktop E2E runs as a job inside ci-e2e.yml (via workflow_call).
245	          # Check ci-e2e.yml runs for the "Desktop E2E" job — same approach as Web E2E.
246	          # When ci-e2e.yml calls desktop-e2e.yml, the job names are composed as
247	          # "Desktop E2E / Desktop E2E (<platform>)" — contains("Desktop E2E") matches.
248	
249	          # Poll for CI E2E orchestrator run at HEAD to complete
250	          MAX_ATTEMPTS=40
251	          POLL_INTERVAL=30
252	          echo "Waiting for CI E2E run to complete (polling every ${POLL_INTERVAL}s, max ${MAX_ATTEMPTS} attempts)..."
253	
254	          for attempt in $(seq 1 $MAX_ATTEMPTS); do
255	            E2E_CONCLUSION=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
256	              --workflow=ci-e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=10 \
257	              --json headSha,conclusion,status,databaseId \
258	              --jq "[.[] | select(.headSha == \"${MAIN_SHA}\")][0].conclusion // empty")
259	
260	            if [ -n "$E2E_CONCLUSION" ]; then
261	              echo "CI E2E completed (conclusion: ${E2E_CONCLUSION}) — checking Desktop E2E job"
262	
263	              E2E_RUN_ID=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
264	                --workflow=ci-e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=10 \
265	                --json headSha,databaseId \
266	                --jq "[.[] | select(.headSha == \"${MAIN_SHA}\")][0].databaseId")
267	              break
268	            fi
269	
270	            if [ "$attempt" -eq "$MAX_ATTEMPTS" ]; then
271	              echo "::error::CI E2E run did not complete within $(( MAX_ATTEMPTS * POLL_INTERVAL / 60 )) minutes for main (${MAIN_SHA})."
272	              exit 1
273	            fi
274	
275	            echo "Attempt ${attempt}/${MAX_ATTEMPTS}: CI E2E still running, waiting ${POLL_INTERVAL}s..."
276	            sleep $POLL_INTERVAL
277	          done
278	
279	          # Check for Desktop E2E jobs with successful conclusion
280	          if ! DESKTOP_E2E_RAN=$(gh api "repos/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${E2E_RUN_ID}/jobs" \
281	            --jq '[.jobs[] | select(.name | contains("Desktop E2E")) | select(.conclusion == "success")] | length'); then
282	            echo "::error::Failed to query jobs for CI E2E run ${E2E_RUN_ID} (API error)"
283	            exit 1
284	          fi
285	
286	          if [ "${DESKTOP_E2E_RAN:-0}" -gt 0 ]; then
287	            echo "Desktop E2E: passed (${DESKTOP_E2E_RAN} platform(s) at HEAD, in CI E2E run ${E2E_RUN_ID})"
288	            exit 0
289	          fi
290	
291	          echo "CI E2E completed but Desktop E2E was skipped or failed in latest run."
292	          echo "Searching for most recent CI E2E run where Desktop E2E passed..."
293	
294	          RECENT_RUN_ID=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \
295	            --workflow=ci-e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=20 \
296	            --json databaseId,status \
297	            --jq '[.[] | select(.status == "completed")] | .[].databaseId' \
298	            | while read -r rid; do
299	                if ! DESKTOP_COUNT=$(gh api "repos/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${rid}/jobs" \
300	                  --jq '[.jobs[] | select(.name | contains("Desktop E2E")) | select(.conclusion == "success")] | length'); then
301	                  echo "::warning::Failed to query jobs for run ${rid}, skipping" >&2
302	                  continue
303	                fi
304	                if [ "${DESKTOP_COUNT:-0}" -gt 0 ]; then
305	                  echo "$rid"
306	                  break
307	                fi
308	              done)
309	
310	          if [ -z "$RECENT_RUN_ID" ]; then
311	            echo "::error::No recent CI E2E run found where Desktop E2E tests passed."
312	            exit 1
313	          fi
314	          echo "Desktop E2E: passed (in CI E2E run ${RECENT_RUN_ID})"
315	
316	      - name: Skip Desktop E2E (no desktop changes)
317	        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--') && needs.detect-changes.outputs.desktop != 'true'
318	        run: echo "No desktop-related changes since last release — skipping Desktop E2E gate"
319	
320	      - name: Summary
321	        if: startsWith(github.head_ref, 'release-please--')
322	        env:
323	          WEB_CHANGED: ${{ needs.detect-changes.outputs.web }}
324	          DESKTOP_CHANGED: ${{ needs.detect-changes.outputs.desktop }}
325	        run: |
326	          echo "Web changes: ${WEB_CHANGED}, Desktop changes: ${DESKTOP_CHANGED}"
327	          echo "All applicable E2E gates passed — release is safe to merge."
328	
```

{
  "command": "git add .github/workflows/release-gate.yml && git commit -m \"$(cat <<'EOF'\nfix(ci): check E2E jobs within ci-e2e.yml, not overall status or standalone runs\n\nTwo bugs in release-gate.yml:\n\n1. Web E2E verification checked the CI E2E orchestrator's overall\n   conclusion before checking the Web E2E job. If Desktop E2E failed,\n   the orchestrator failed, blocking the web gate even though Web E2E\n   passed. Fix: skip the overall conclusion check and inspect the\n   specific Web E2E job directly.\n\n2. Desktop E2E verification searched for standalone desktop-e2e.yml\n   workflow runs. But when ci-e2e.yml calls desktop-e2e.yml via\n   workflow_call, it doesn't create separate workflow runs — the jobs\n   appear within the ci-e2e.yml run. Fix: check for \"Desktop E2E\" jobs\n   inside ci-e2e.yml runs (same pattern as the web check).\n\nAlso fixed the web fallback search to not filter by overall run\nconclusion, so it can find passing Web E2E jobs even in runs where\nother jobs failed.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.6 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>\nEOF\n)\"",
  "description": "Commit release-gate fix"
}

> AGENT

Summary of the two fixes:

1. **Web E2E** (lines 194-196): Removed the `if E2E_CONCLUSION != success` gate that checked the CI E2E orchestrator's *overall* result. Now it goes straight to inspecting the "Web E2E" job within the run. Also fixed the fallback search to not require overall run success — it now checks any completed ci-e2e.yml run for a passing Web E2E job.

2. **Desktop E2E** (lines 235-314): Completely rewritten to mirror the web approach — finds the ci-e2e.yml run at HEAD, checks for "Desktop E2E" jobs within it, and falls back to searching recent ci-e2e.yml runs. No longer searches for standalone `desktop-e2e.yml` workflow runs (which don't exist when triggered via `workflow_call`).

Both checks are now consistent: find ci-e2e.yml orchestrator run → check the specific job by name → fall back to recent runs if skipped. Want me to push and create the PR?

{

WEB_E2E_RAN=$(gh api \"repos/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${E2E_RUN_ID}/jobs\" \\\n            --jq '[.jobs[] | select(.name | contains(\"Web E2E\")) | select(.conclusion == \"success\")] | length'); then\n            echo \"::error::Failed to query jobs for CI E2E run ${E2E_RUN_ID} (API error)\"\n            exit 1\n          fi\n\n          if [ \"${WEB_E2E_RAN:-0}\" -eq 0 ]; then\n            echo \"CI E2E passed but Web E2E was skipped (no web changes in latest push).\"\n            echo \"Searching for most recent CI E2E run where Web E2E actually executed...\"\n\n            RECENT_RUN_ID=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \\\n              --workflow=ci-e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=20 \\\n              --json databaseId,status \\\n              --jq '[.[] | select(.status == \"completed\")] | .[].databaseId' \\\n              | while read -r rid; do\n                  if ! WEB_COUNT=$(gh api \"repos/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${rid}/jobs\" \\\n                    --jq '[.jobs[] | select(.name | contains(\"Web E2E\")) | select(.conclusion == \"success\")] | length'); then\n                    echo \"::warning::Failed to query jobs for run ${rid}, skipping\" >&2\n                    continue\n                  fi\n                  if [ \"${WEB_COUNT:-0}\" -gt 0 ]; then\n                    echo \"$rid\"\n                    break\n                  fi\n                done)\n\n            if [ -z \"$RECENT_RUN_ID\" ]; then\n              echo \"::error::No recent CI E2E run found where Web E2E tests actually executed.\"\n              exit 1\n            fi\n            echo \"Web E2E: passed (in CI E2E run ${RECENT_RUN_ID})\"\n          else\n            echo \"Web E2E: passed (at HEAD)\"\n          fi",
  "new_string": "          # Don't check overall CI E2E conclusion — it may fail due to\n          # Desktop E2E issues while Web E2E passed. Check the job directly.\n\n          # Verify the Web E2E job actually ran (not skipped due to no web changes).\n          # When ci-e2e.yml calls web-e2e.yml via workflow_call, the job name is\n          # composed as \"Web E2E / Web E2E Tests\" — contains(\"Web E2E\") matches both.\n          #\n          # Distinguish three states: passed, failed, or skipped/absent.\n          # Only fall back to older runs when skipped — fail fast on failure.\n          if ! WEB_JOBS_JSON=$(gh api \"repos/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${E2E_RUN_ID}/jobs\" \\\n            --jq '[.jobs[] | select(.name | contains(\"Web E2E\"))]'); then\n            echo \"::error::Failed to query jobs for CI E2E run ${E2E_RUN_ID} (API error)\"\n            exit 1\n          fi\n\n          WEB_TOTAL=$(echo \"$WEB_JOBS_JSON\" | jq 'length')\n          WEB_SUCCESS=$(echo \"$WEB_JOBS_JSON\" | jq '[.[] | select(.conclusion == \"success\")] | length')\n          WEB_FAILED=$(echo \"$WEB_JOBS_JSON\" | jq '[.[] | select(.conclusion != \"success\" and .conclusion != \"skipped\" and .conclusion != null)] | length')\n\n          if [ \"${WEB_TOTAL:-0}\" -gt 0 ] && [ \"${WEB_FAILED:-0}\" -gt 0 ]; then\n            echo \"::error::Web E2E ran but failed at HEAD (${WEB_FAILED} failed job(s) in CI E2E run ${E2E_RUN_ID}).\"\n            exit 1\n          fi\n\n          if [ \"${WEB_SUCCESS:-0}\" -gt 0 ]; then\n            echo \"Web E2E: passed (at HEAD, in CI E2E run ${E2E_RUN_ID})\"\n          else\n            echo \"Web E2E was skipped in latest CI E2E run (no web changes in that push).\"\n            echo \"Searching for most recent CI E2E run where Web E2E actually executed...\"\n\n            RECENT_RUN_ID=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \\\n              --workflow=ci-e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=20 \\\n              --json databaseId,status \\\n              --jq '[.[] | select(.status == \"completed\")] | .[].databaseId' \\\n              | while read -r rid; do\n                  if ! JOBS_JSON=$(gh api \"repos/${{ github.repository }}/actions/runs/${rid}/jobs\" \\\n                    --jq '[.jobs[] | select(.name | contains(\"Web E2E\"))]'); then\n                    echo \"::warning::Failed to query jobs for run ${rid}, skipping\" >&2\n                    continue\n                  fi\n                  TOTAL=$(echo \"$JOBS_JSON\" | jq 'length')\n                  SUCCESS=$(echo \"$JOBS_JSON\" | jq '[.[] | select(.conclusion == \"success\")] | length')\n                  FAILED=$(echo \"$JOBS_JSON\" | jq '[.[] | select(.conclusion != \"success\" and .conclusion != \"skipped\" and .conclusion != null)] | length')\n                  # Only accept runs where Web E2E ran AND all jobs passed\n                  if [ \"${TOTAL:-0}\" -gt 0 ] && [ \"${SUCCESS:-0}\" -gt 0 ] && [ \"${FAILED:-0}\" -eq 0 ]; then\n                    echo \"$rid\"\n                    break\n                  fi\n                done)\n\n            if [ -z \"$RECENT_RUN_ID\" ]; then\n              echo \"::error::No recent CI E2E run found where Web E2E tests passed.\"\n              exit 1\n            fi\n            echo \"Web E2E: passed (in CI E2E run ${RECENT_RUN_ID})\"\n          fi"
}

  "command": "git add .github/workflows/release-gate.yml && git commit -m \"$(cat <<'EOF'\nfix(ci): distinguish failed vs skipped E2E jobs in release gate\n\nAddress review feedback: the gate now distinguishes three states for\neach E2E check (passed/failed/skipped) instead of two (passed/other).\n\n- Failed jobs trigger immediate failure instead of falling back to\n  older runs — prevents masking regressions on main.\n- Desktop E2E matrix: verifies ALL platform jobs passed, not just\n  that at least one succeeded (prevents partial-failure false pass).\n- Fallback to older runs only activates when jobs are absent/skipped.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.6 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>\nEOF\n)\"",
  "description": "Commit review feedback fixes"
}

> AGENT

Both valid — the fallback loops skip past failed runs to find older passing ones, which could mask regressions. The first run that actually ran the E2E jobs should be authoritative. Let me fix both.

> AGENT

The fix needs `exit 1` inside the loop to propagate out of the step. The piped `while` subshell means `exit 1` only exits the subshell — we need process substitution (`< <(...)`) instead so the loop runs in the main shell.

{
  "command": "git add .github/workflows/release-gate.yml && git commit -m \"$(cat <<'EOF'\nfix(ci): make fallback E2E search authoritative on first match\n\nThe fallback loops that search older ci-e2e.yml runs now treat the\nfirst run that actually contains the E2E jobs as authoritative: if\nthat run failed, fail the gate immediately instead of scanning further\nback for an older passing run. This prevents masking regressions.\n\nAlso switched from piped while-loop (subshell) to process substitution\nso that exit 1 inside the loop propagates to the step.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.6 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>\nEOF\n)\" && git push",
  "description": "Commit and push authoritative fallback fix"
}
```

Perfect! I have all the information needed. Let me compile the complete analysis:

## Complete Package Analysis & Build Order

### All Internal @cipherbox Packages Summary

| Package | Has Build Script | @cipherbox Dependencies |
|---------|------------------|--------------------------|
| @cipherbox/api-client | Yes (tsup) | None |
| @cipherbox/crypto | Yes (tsup) | None (only dev: @cipherbox/core) |
| @cipherbox/core | Yes (tsup) | @cipherbox/crypto |
| @cipherbox/sdk-core | Yes (tsup) | @cipherbox/crypto, @cipherbox/core, @cipherbox/api-client |
| @cipherbox/sdk | Yes (tsup) | @cipherbox/crypto, @cipherbox/core, @cipherbox/api-client, @cipherbox/sdk-core |
| @cipherbox/web | Yes (tsc + vite) | All five packages above |

### Correct Build Order (Dependency Graph)

Based on the dependency analysis, here is the **correct build order**:

1. **@cipherbox/api-client** (no dependencies)
2. **@cipherbox/crypto** (no dependencies, only dev dependency on @cipherbox/core)
3. **@cipherbox/core** (depends on @cipherbox/crypto)
4. **@cipherbox/sdk-core** (depends on @cipherbox/crypto, @cipherbox/core, @cipherbox/api-client)
5. **@cipherbox/sdk** (depends on @cipherbox/crypto, @cipherbox/core, @cipherbox/api-client, @cipherbox/sdk-core)
6. **@cipherbox/web** (depends on all five packages)

### Notes

- All packages have a `build` script (using `tsup` for libraries, `vite` for the web app)
- @cipherbox/crypto has @cipherbox/core only as a **dev dependency**, so it can be built independently
- The web app uses `tsc -b` (TypeScript build mode) which can handle transitive dependencies, but explicit build ordering ensures correct compilation
- All dependencies are using `workspace:*` protocol, indicating a monorepo setup

**Files referenced:**
- /Users/michael/Code/cipher-box-ci-debug/apps/web/package.json
- /Users/michael/Code/cipher-box-ci-debug/packages/api-client/package.json
- /Users/michael/Code/cipher-box-ci-debug/packages/core/package.json
- /Users/michael/Code/cipher-box-ci-debug/packages/crypto/package.json
- /Users/michael/Code/cipher-box-ci-debug/packages/sdk-core/package.json
- /Users/michael/Code/cipher-box-ci-debug/packages/sdk/package.json
```

> AGENT

The build command should be:

```
pnpm install --frozen-lockfile && pnpm --filter api-client build && pnpm --filter crypto build && pnpm --filter core build && pnpm --filter sdk-core build && pnpm --filter sdk build && pnpm --filter web build
```

The dependency chain is: `api-client` + `crypto` (no deps) → `core` (needs crypto) → `sdk-core` (needs crypto, core, api-client) → `sdk` (needs all four) → `web` (needs everything).

          "body": "The Desktop E2E fallback loop has the same issue as the Web fallback: if the \"Desktop E2E\" jobs exist in a run but are all `skipped`, `TOTAL` will be > 0 and `SUCCESS != TOTAL` will immediately fail the gate. Since the intent is to fall back only when Desktop E2E was skipped/absent, the loop should continue past runs where all matching Desktop E2E jobs have `.conclusion == \"skipped\"`, and only evaluate pass/fail once at least one non-skipped Desktop E2E job is present.",

> AGENT

Valid point — if all Desktop E2E jobs are `skipped` in a run, TOTAL > 0 but SUCCESS = 0, causing a false failure instead of continuing the search. Need to also check the Web fallback for the same issue.

> AGENT

Both fallbacks have the same issue. Fix: filter to only non-skipped jobs before evaluating pass/fail, and continue if all are skipped.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01DqKT3EaTvhXGirBTzMfgNZ
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box-ci-debug/.github/workflows/release-gate.yml",
  "old_string": "              TOTAL=$(echo \"$JOBS_JSON\" | jq 'length')\n              # Skip runs where Web E2E was absent/skipped\n              [ \"${TOTAL:-0}\" -eq 0 ] && continue\n              # First run with Web E2E jobs is authoritative — pass or fail\n              SUCCESS=$(echo \"$JOBS_JSON\" | jq '[.[] | select(.conclusion == \"success\")] | length')\n              if [ \"${SUCCESS:-0}\" -ne \"${TOTAL:-0}\" ]; then\n                echo \"::error::Most recent CI E2E run with Web E2E (run ${rid}) failed (${SUCCESS}/${TOTAL} passed).\"\n                exit 1\n              fi",
  "new_string": "              # Filter to non-skipped jobs — all-skipped means E2E didn't run\n              ACTIVE=$(echo \"$JOBS_JSON\" | jq '[.[] | select(.conclusion != \"skipped\" and .conclusion != null)] | length')\n              [ \"${ACTIVE:-0}\" -eq 0 ] && continue\n              # First run with active Web E2E jobs is authoritative — pass or fail\n              SUCCESS=$(echo \"$JOBS_JSON\" | jq '[.[] | select(.conclusion == \"success\")] | length')\n              if [ \"${SUCCESS:-0}\" -ne \"${ACTIVE}\" ]; then\n                echo \"::error::Most recent CI E2E run with Web E2E (run ${rid}) failed (${SUCCESS}/${ACTIVE} passed).\"\n                exit 1\n              fi"
}

{
  "command": "git add .github/workflows/release-gate.yml && git commit -m \"$(cat <<'EOF'\nfix(ci): skip all-skipped E2E jobs in fallback search\n\nIf all matching E2E jobs in a ci-e2e.yml run have conclusion \"skipped\",\nthe fallback should continue searching older runs (E2E didn't actually\nrun). Previously, TOTAL > 0 but SUCCESS == 0 would trigger a false\nfailure. Now counts only non-skipped (active) jobs for the pass/fail\nevaluation.\n\nApplies to both Web and Desktop E2E fallback loops.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.6 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>\nEOF\n)\" && git push",
  "description": "Commit and push skipped-jobs fix"
}

> AGENT

Both are the same issue — `[0]` picks the newest run for the SHA, which could be an in-progress rerun while an older completed run exists. Fix: prefer completed runs.

> AGENT

The fix: sort completed runs first in the jq selector, so a completed run is preferred over an in-progress rerun. Also fix the run ID query to match.

> TOOL

id: toolu_015vsByVnkuTWaHUVMNz13Wk
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box-ci-debug/.github/workflows/release-gate.yml",
  "old_string": "          for attempt in $(seq 1 $MAX_ATTEMPTS); do\n            E2E_CONCLUSION=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \\\n              --workflow=ci-e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=10 \\\n              --json headSha,conclusion,status,databaseId \\\n              --jq \"[.[] | select(.headSha == \\\"${MAIN_SHA}\\\")][0].conclusion // empty\")\n\n            if [ -n \"$E2E_CONCLUSION\" ]; then\n              echo \"CI E2E completed with conclusion: ${E2E_CONCLUSION}\"\n\n              # Grab the run ID for job-level checks below\n              E2E_RUN_ID=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \\\n                --workflow=ci-e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=10 \\\n                --json headSha,databaseId \\\n                --jq \"[.[] | select(.headSha == \\\"${MAIN_SHA}\\\")][0].databaseId\")\n              break\n            fi\n\n            if [ \"$attempt\" -eq \"$MAX_ATTEMPTS\" ]; then\n              echo \"::error::CI E2E run did not complete within $(( MAX_ATTEMPTS * POLL_INTERVAL / 60 )) minutes for main (${MAIN_SHA}).\"\n              exit 1\n            fi\n\n            echo \"Attempt ${attempt}/${MAX_ATTEMPTS}: CI E2E still running, waiting ${POLL_INTERVAL}s...\"\n            sleep $POLL_INTERVAL\n          done",
  "new_string": "          for attempt in $(seq 1 $MAX_ATTEMPTS); do\n            # Prefer a completed run over an in-progress rerun for the same SHA.\n            # Sort completed runs first so reruns don't block the gate.\n            RUN_DATA=$(gh run list --repo ${{ github.repository }} \\\n              --workflow=ci-e2e.yml --branch=main --limit=10 \\\n              --json headSha,conclusion,status,databaseId \\\n              --jq \"[.[] | select(.headSha == \\\"${MAIN_SHA}\\\")] | sort_by(.status == \\\"completed\\\" | not) | .[0]\")\n\n            E2E_CONCLUSION=$(echo \"$RUN_DATA\" | jq -r '.conclusion // empty')\n\n            if [ -n \"$E2E_CONCLUSION\" ] && [ \"$E2E_CONCLUSION\" != \"null\" ]; then\n              E2E_RUN_ID=$(echo \"$RUN_DATA\" | jq -r '.databaseId')\n              echo \"CI E2E completed with conclusion: ${E2E_CONCLUSION} (run ${E2E_RUN_ID})\"\n              break\n            fi\n\n            if [ \"$attempt\" -eq \"$MAX_ATTEMPTS\" ]; then\n              echo \"::error::CI E2E run did not complete within $(( MAX_ATTEMPTS * POLL_INTERVAL / 60 )) minutes for main (${MAIN_SHA}).\"\n              exit 1\n            fi\n\n            echo \"Attempt ${attempt}/${MAX_ATTEMPTS}: CI E2E still running, waiting ${POLL_INTERVAL}s...\"\n            sleep $POLL_INTERVAL\n          done"
}

{
  "command": "git add .github/workflows/release-gate.yml && git commit -m \"$(cat <<'EOF'\nfix(ci): prefer completed CI E2E run over in-progress rerun\n\nThe polling loops selected [0] from runs matching the HEAD SHA, which\npicks the newest run. If a rerun is in-progress while an older\ncompleted run exists, the gate would wait unnecessarily (or time out).\n\nNow sorts completed runs first via jq sort_by, so a completed run is\nused immediately even if a rerun is in-progress. Also consolidates\nthe conclusion + run ID into a single API call.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.6 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>\nEOF\n)\" && git push",
  "description": "Commit and push rerun preference fix"
}

`@FSM1` The refactor is well-structured — the three-state (passed / failed / skipped) distinction, fail-fast on failure vs. fall-back on skip, and the unified pattern for both suites are all clear improvements. A few items worth attention:

---

### 🔴 Timeout budget mismatch

The job's `timeout-minutes: 50` but each poll loop can run up to `MAX_ATTEMPTS=90 × POLL_INTERVAL=30s = 45 min`. When both Web E2E *and* Desktop E2E steps run, the Desktop step's poll exits on attempt 1 (the run is already complete), so the total is ~45 min + overhead — fine.

But when **only Desktop E2E** runs (no web changes detected, so the Web step is skipped), the Desktop step must do the full 45-min poll on its own. With 50 min available, there's only ~5 min of margin for everything else in the job. If the desktop suite runs long, the job timeout will fire before the poll completes and the gate exits without a useful error message.

**Suggested fix:** raise `timeout-minutes` to at least `60`, or keep a single poll loop that is shared by both checks.

---

### 🟡 `E2E_RUN_ID` not validated after the poll loop

After both poll loops (one in each step), `E2E_RUN_ID` is set with:

```bash
E2E_RUN_ID=$(echo "$RUN_DATA" | jq -r '.databaseId')
```

If `$RUN_DATA` is unexpectedly empty or `.databaseId` is `null`, this becomes the literal string `"null"` and the subsequent `gh api "…/actions/runs/null/jobs"` call fails at the API level with a confusing 404 rather than a clear error. A guard after the loop would help:

```bash
if [ -z "$E2E_RUN_ID" ] || [ "$E2E_RUN_ID" = "null" ]; then
  echo "::error::Could not determine CI E2E run ID for ${MAIN_SHA}"
  exit 1
fi
```

---

### 🟡 Missing `:-0` default in Desktop success comparison

```bash
# Web (consistent)
if [ "${WEB_TOTAL:-0}" -gt 0 ] && [ "${WEB_FAILED:-0}" -gt 0 ]; then
...
if [ "${WEB_SUCCESS:-0}" -gt 0 ]; then

# Desktop — DESKTOP_TOTAL has no default in the equality test
if [ "${DESKTOP_SUCCESS:-0}" -gt 0 ] && [ "$DESKTOP_SUCCESS" -eq "$DESKTOP_TOTAL" ]; then
```

`$DESKTOP_TOTAL` should be `${DESKTOP_TOTAL:-0}` for consistency (jq `length` always returns a number, so this won't cause a real bug, but it's an inconsistency that can bite during future edits).

---

### 🟢 Everything else looks good

- The `sort_by(.status == "completed" | not)` logic is correct — `false` (completed) sorts before `true` (in-progress), so reruns don't block the gate. ✓  
- The fallback `ACTIVE > 0` guard correctly skips all-skipped runs (including the current `E2E_RUN_ID`) without needing an explicit exclusion. ✓  
- Desktop matrix "all must pass" (`SUCCESS == ACTIVE`) is strictly correct and prevents partial-success false positives. ✓  
- The `WEB_FAILED` filter (`!= "success" and != "skipped" and != null`) correctly handles all terminal failure states within a completed run. ✓  
- Duplicate poll loops — acceptable for GitHub Actions bash; extracting a helper function would be cleaner but is not a blocking concern.

---

Learning: In FSM1/cipher-box (`release-gate.yml`), `.github/workflows/ci-e2e.yml` is intentionally excluded from both `WEB_PATTERNS` and `DESKTOP_PATTERNS` in the release gate's change-detection step. Reason: `ci-e2e.yml` self-includes in its own `dorny/paths-filter` for both `web` and `desktop`, so any change to the orchestrator still triggers both E2E suites on `main` pushes. Including it in the release gate patterns would cause a deadlock: the gate detects "desktop changed" but `ci-e2e.yml`'s narrower per-push filter may not schedule a Desktop E2E job, leaving no run for the gate to find. The release gate only needs to block on actual app-code changes, not on CI config changes. Do NOT flag the absence of `ci-e2e.yml` from the release gate pattern lists.

Learning: In `tests/e2e-desktop/scripts/*.{sh,ps1}` (FUSE E2E tests): Fixed sleep-based waits after file mutations are an inherent limitation of FUSE-mount testing. The FUSE daemon provides no IPC signal or status endpoint to indicate when a debounced IPNS metadata publish completes. Tests use conservative fixed waits (e.g., 8s for initial publish accounting for 1.5s debounce + 10s safety valve, 15s for conflict resolution) and can be increased if CI proves flaky. Eliminating sleep-based waits would require exposing an IPC readiness signal from the FUSE daemon.
```

          "body": "The polling loop assigns `RUN_DATA=$(gh run list ...)` and immediately parses it with `jq`, but it never checks whether `gh run list` succeeded or returned valid JSON. }
